Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in translation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 393 and the invariant I(A,B) = 950 mod 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by translation generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 393, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 950. QED.

Theorem 213.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)
